    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    min-width: 44px;
    width: 44px;
    min-height: 44px;

  }

  .schematic-cart-feedback {

<script>
  document.querySelectorAll('.interactable-schematic-boxes[data-boxes-overlay]').forEach(function(el) {
    var html = el.getAttribute('data-boxes-overlay');
    if (html) {
      el.innerHTML = html;
      el.removeAttribute('data-boxes-overlay');
    }
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('.interactable-schematic-overlay[data-links-overlay]').forEach(function(el) {
    var html = el.getAttribute('data-links-overlay');
    if (html) {
      el.innerHTML = html;
      el.removeAttribute('data-links-overlay');
    }
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('.schematic-canvas .interactable-schematic-overlay a').forEach(function(link) {
    link.setAttribute('draggable', 'false');
  });

  (function initSchematicCanvas() {
    var roots = document.querySelectorAll('[data-schematic-canvas]');
    if (!roots.length) return;

    roots.forEach(function(root) {
      var stage = root.querySelector('.schematic-canvas-stage');
      var viewport = root.querySelector('[data-canvas-viewport]');
      var world = root.querySelector('[data-canvas-world]');
      var grid = root.querySelector('[data-canvas-grid]');
      var pages = Array.prototype.slice.call(root.querySelectorAll('[data-canvas-page]'));
      if (!viewport || !world || !grid || !pages.length) return;

      var zoomInBtn = root.querySelector('[data-canvas-zoom-in]');
      var zoomOutBtn = root.querySelector('[data-canvas-zoom-out]');
      var zoomResetBtn = root.querySelector('[data-canvas-zoom-reset]');
      var scrollDownBtn = root.querySelector('[data-canvas-scroll-down]');
      var fsEnterBtn = root.querySelector('[data-canvas-fs-enter]');
      var fsExitBtns = Array.prototype.slice.call(root.querySelectorAll('[data-canvas-fs-exit]'));

      var MIN_SCALE = 0.08;
      var MAX_SCALE = 6;
      var ZOOM_STEP = 1.2;
      var DRAG_THRESHOLD = 6;
      var FIT_PADDING = 24;
      var GRID_COLUMNS = 2;

      function baseGap() {
        return window.matchMedia('(max-width: 768px)').matches ? 20 : 32;
      }

      var scale = 1;
      var tx = 0;
      var ty = 0;
      var fitScale = 1;
      var baseWidth = 1;
      var baseHeight = 1;
      var pointers = new Map();
      var pinchStartDist = 0;
      var pinchStartScale = 1;
      var pinchWorldX = 0;
      var pinchWorldY = 0;
      var panActive = false;
      var panMoved = false;
      var panPointerId = null;
      var panStartX = 0;
      var panStartY = 0;
      var panOriginTx = 0;
      var panOriginTy = 0;
      var suppressClick = false;
      var isFullscreen = false;
      var pressOverlayTarget = null;

      function clamp(n, min, max) {
        return Math.max(min, Math.min(max, n));
      }

      function syncFullscreenUi() {
        if (!stage) return;
        stage.classList.toggle('is-fullscreen', isFullscreen);
        document.body.classList.toggle('schematic-canvas-fs-open', isFullscreen);
        if (fsEnterBtn) {
          if (isFullscreen) fsEnterBtn.setAttribute('hidden', '');
          else fsEnterBtn.removeAttribute('hidden');
        }
        fsExitBtns.forEach(function(btn) {
          if (isFullscreen) btn.removeAttribute('hidden');
          else btn.setAttribute('hidden', '');
        });
        requestAnimationFrame(function() {
          fitToViewport(true);
        });
      }

      function getNativeFullscreenElement() {
        return document.fullscreenElement || document.webkitFullscreenElement || null;
      }

      function requestNativeFullscreen(el) {
        if (!el) return Promise.resolve(false);
        var req = el.requestFullscreen || el.webkitRequestFullscreen;
        if (!req) return Promise.resolve(false);
        try {
          var result = req.call(el);
          if (result && typeof result.then === 'function') {
            return result.then(function() { return true; }).catch(function() { return false; });
          }
          return Promise.resolve(true);
        } catch (err) {
          return Promise.resolve(false);
        }
      }

      function exitNativeFullscreen() {
        var exit = document.exitFullscreen || document.webkitExitFullscreen;
        if (!exit || !getNativeFullscreenElement()) return Promise.resolve();
        try {
          var result = exit.call(document);
          if (result && typeof result.then === 'function') return result.catch(function() {});
        } catch (err) {}
        return Promise.resolve();
      }

      function enterFullscreen() {
        if (!isFullscreen) {
          isFullscreen = true;
          syncFullscreenUi();
        }
        // Attempt real browser fullscreen when allowed (user gesture / F key).
        if (getNativeFullscreenElement() !== stage) {
          requestNativeFullscreen(stage).then(function() {
            mountOverlayUi();
          });
        } else {
          mountOverlayUi();
        }
      }

      function exitFullscreen() {
        if (!isFullscreen && getNativeFullscreenElement() !== stage) return;
        isFullscreen = false;
        syncFullscreenUi();
        exitNativeFullscreen().then(function() {
          mountOverlayUi();
        });
        mountOverlayUi();
      }

      function toggleFullscreen() {
        if (isFullscreen) exitFullscreen();
        else enterFullscreen();
      }

      function isProductModalOpen() {
        var tip = document.getElementById('schematic-tooltip');
        return !!(tip && tip.classList.contains('tooltip-mobile') && tip.style.display === 'block');
      }

      function overlayUiNodes() {
        return [
          document.getElementById('schematic-tooltip-backdrop'),
          document.getElementById('schematic-tooltip'),
          document.getElementById('schematic-cart-feedback'),
          document.getElementById('schematic-cart-feedback-fail')
        ].filter(Boolean);
      }

      // Native fullscreen only shows descendants of the fullscreen element.
      // Product modals live on body by default, so reparent them into the stage.
      function mountOverlayUi() {
        if (!stage) return;
        var host = getNativeFullscreenElement() === stage ? stage : document.body;
        overlayUiNodes().forEach(function(el) {
          if (el.parentNode !== host) host.appendChild(el);
        });
      }

      function pageImages() {
        return pages.map(function(page) {
          return page.querySelector('.interactable-schematic-image');
        }).filter(Boolean);
      }

      var LOAD_MARGIN_PX = 220;
      var UNLOAD_MARGIN_PX = 720;
      // Only a couple of pages should ever hold a full-res bitmap.
      var MAX_FULL_RES_MOBILE = 1;
      var MAX_FULL_RES_DESKTOP = 2;
      var syncImagesRaf = 0;

      function maxFullResPages() {
        return window.matchMedia('(max-width: 768px)').matches
          ? MAX_FULL_RES_MOBILE
          : MAX_FULL_RES_DESKTOP;
      }

      function layoutSize(img) {
        var w = Number(img.getAttribute('data-layout-w')) || 0;
        var h = Number(img.getAttribute('data-layout-h')) || 0;
        if (w > 0 && h > 0) return { w: w, h: h };
        w = img.naturalWidth || Number(img.getAttribute('width')) || 2400;
        h = img.naturalHeight || Number(img.getAttribute('height')) || Math.round(w * 1.294);
        return { w: w, h: h };
      }

      function ensureLayoutStyles() {
        var imgs = pageImages();
        for (var i = 0; i < imgs.length; i++) {
          var size = layoutSize(imgs[i]);
          imgs[i].style.width = size.w + 'px';
          imgs[i].style.height = size.h + 'px';
        }
      }

      function measureBaseSize() {
        ensureLayoutStyles();
        var imgs = pageImages();
        var gap = baseGap();
        var width = 0;
        var height = 0;
        var row = 0;
        while (row < imgs.length) {
          var rowWidth = 0;
          var rowHeight = 0;
          var colsInRow = 0;
          for (var col = 0; col < GRID_COLUMNS && row + col < imgs.length; col++) {
            var size = layoutSize(imgs[row + col]);
            if (colsInRow > 0) rowWidth += gap;
            rowWidth += size.w;
            if (size.h > rowHeight) rowHeight = size.h;
            colsInRow += 1;
          }
          if (colsInRow > 0) {
            if (height > 0) height += gap;
            height += rowHeight;
            if (rowWidth > width) width = rowWidth;
          }
          row += GRID_COLUMNS;
        }
        if (!width || !height) {
          width = Math.max(grid.scrollWidth, 1);
          height = Math.max(grid.scrollHeight, 1);
        }
        baseWidth = Math.max(1, width);
        baseHeight = Math.max(1, height);
      }

      function urlForTier(img, tier) {
        return img.getAttribute('data-src-' + tier)
          || img.getAttribute('data-src-lg')
          || img.getAttribute('data-src-sm')
          || '';
      }

      function pageDistanceState(pageEl) {
        var pageRect = pageEl.getBoundingClientRect();
        var viewRect = viewport.getBoundingClientRect();
        var dx = 0;
        var dy = 0;
        if (pageRect.right < viewRect.left) dx = viewRect.left - pageRect.right;
        else if (pageRect.left > viewRect.right) dx = pageRect.left - viewRect.right;
        if (pageRect.bottom < viewRect.top) dy = viewRect.top - pageRect.bottom;
        else if (pageRect.top > viewRect.bottom) dy = pageRect.top - viewRect.bottom;
        return Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
      }

      // Distance from page center to viewport center — prefer the page you're looking at.
      function pageCenterDistance(pageEl) {
        var pageRect = pageEl.getBoundingClientRect();
        var viewRect = viewport.getBoundingClientRect();
        var dx = ((pageRect.left + pageRect.right) / 2) - ((viewRect.left + viewRect.right) / 2);
        var dy = ((pageRect.top + pageRect.bottom) / 2) - ((viewRect.top + viewRect.bottom) / 2);
        return Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
      }

      function pageContainsViewCenter(pageEl) {
        var pageRect = pageEl.getBoundingClientRect();
        var viewRect = viewport.getBoundingClientRect();
        var cx = (viewRect.left + viewRect.right) / 2;
        var cy = (viewRect.top + viewRect.bottom) / 2;
        return cx >= pageRect.left && cx <= pageRect.right
          && cy >= pageRect.top && cy <= pageRect.bottom;
      }

      // How large a page appears on screen — used to decide preview vs full-res.
      function pageCloseness(pageEl) {
        var pageRect = pageEl.getBoundingClientRect();
        var viewRect = viewport.getBoundingClientRect();
        var overlapW = Math.min(pageRect.right, viewRect.right) - Math.max(pageRect.left, viewRect.left);
        var overlapH = Math.min(pageRect.bottom, viewRect.bottom) - Math.max(pageRect.top, viewRect.top);
        var overlaps = overlapW > 0 && overlapH > 0;
        var viewArea = Math.max(viewRect.width * viewRect.height, 1);
        var visibleArea = overlaps ? overlapW * overlapH : 0;
        var coverage = visibleArea / viewArea;
        var pageFill = overlaps
          ? Math.min(pageRect.width / Math.max(viewRect.width, 1), pageRect.height / Math.max(viewRect.height, 1))
          : 0;
        return {
          overlaps: overlaps,
          coverage: coverage,
          pageFill: pageFill,
          displayW: Math.max(pageRect.width, 0),
          underCenter: overlaps && pageContainsViewCenter(pageEl)
        };
      }

      // Enter full-res when the page is large enough on screen; exit with hysteresis.
      function isZoomedEnoughForFullRes(pageEl, img) {
        var c = pageCloseness(pageEl);
        if (!c.overlaps) return false;
        var isFull = (img.dataset.tier || '') === 'lg';
        if (isFull) {
          return c.coverage >= 0.14 || c.pageFill >= 0.32 || c.displayW >= 520;
        }
        return c.coverage >= 0.26 || c.pageFill >= 0.48 || c.displayW >= 700;
      }

      function unloadPageImage(img) {
        if (!img) return;
        // Only free memory for offscreen pages. Never leave an on-screen slot empty.
        img._loadingToken = (img._loadingToken || 0) + 1;
        img._loadingUrl = '';
        img.dataset.tier = '';
        if (img.hasAttribute('src')) img.removeAttribute('src');
      }

      function loadPageImage(img, tier) {
        if (!img || !tier) return;
        var url = urlForTier(img, tier);
        if (!url) return;
        var currentTier = img.dataset.tier || '';
        if (currentTier === tier && img.getAttribute('src') === url) return;
        if (img._loadingUrl === url) return;

        var token = (img._loadingToken || 0) + 1;
        img._loadingToken = token;
        img._loadingUrl = url;

        var probe = new Image();
        probe.decoding = 'async';
        var settled = false;

        function commitSwap() {
          if (settled || img._loadingToken !== token) return;
          settled = true;
          // Keep the current bitmap painted until the next frame after decode,
          // then swap src from cache so the preview doesn't blank first.
          requestAnimationFrame(function() {
            if (img._loadingToken !== token) return;
            img.src = url;
            img.dataset.tier = tier;
            img._loadingUrl = '';
          });
        }

        function afterLoaded() {
          if (settled || img._loadingToken !== token) return;
          if (typeof probe.decode === 'function') {
            probe.decode().then(commitSwap).catch(commitSwap);
          } else {
            commitSwap();
          }
        }

        probe.onload = afterLoaded;
        probe.onerror = function() {
          if (settled || img._loadingToken !== token) return;
          img._loadingUrl = '';
          if (tier === 'lg') loadPageImage(img, 'sm');
        };
        probe.src = url;
        // Cached images may already be complete before onload fires.
        if (probe.complete && probe.naturalWidth) afterLoaded();
      }

      function syncPageImages() {
        var ranked = [];
        for (var i = 0; i < pages.length; i++) {
          var pageEl = pages[i];
          var img = pageEl.querySelector('.interactable-schematic-image');
          if (!img) continue;
          var closeness = pageCloseness(pageEl);
          ranked.push({
            pageEl: pageEl,
            img: img,
            dist: pageDistanceState(pageEl),
            centerDist: pageCenterDistance(pageEl),
            overlaps: closeness.overlaps,
            underCenter: closeness.underCenter
          });
        }

        var fullBudget = maxFullResPages();
        var loadCutoff = LOAD_MARGIN_PX;
        if (scale > fitScale * 1.35) loadCutoff = Math.max(LOAD_MARGIN_PX, 320);

        // Prefer the page under the viewport center (not DOM order / top-edge winners).
        var fullImgs = [];
        var fullCandidates = ranked.filter(function(entry) {
          return entry.overlaps && isZoomedEnoughForFullRes(entry.pageEl, entry.img);
        });
        fullCandidates.sort(function(a, b) {
          if (a.underCenter !== b.underCenter) return a.underCenter ? -1 : 1;
          return a.centerDist - b.centerDist;
        });
        for (var f = 0; f < fullCandidates.length && f < fullBudget; f++) {
          fullImgs.push(fullCandidates[f].img);
        }

        for (var j = 0; j < ranked.length; j++) {
          var entry = ranked[j];
          var onOrNear = entry.overlaps || entry.dist <= loadCutoff;

          // Offscreen and far: free memory. On-screen pages are never blanked.
          if (!entry.overlaps && entry.dist > UNLOAD_MARGIN_PX) {
            unloadPageImage(entry.img);
            continue;
          }

          if (!onOrNear) {
            // Between near and far: keep a preview if we already have one, else leave alone.
            if ((entry.img.dataset.tier || '') === 'lg') loadPageImage(entry.img, 'sm');
            continue;
          }

          if (fullImgs.indexOf(entry.img) !== -1) loadPageImage(entry.img, 'lg');
          else loadPageImage(entry.img, 'sm');
        }
      }

      function scheduleSyncPageImages() {
        if (syncImagesRaf) return;
        syncImagesRaf = requestAnimationFrame(function() {
          syncImagesRaf = 0;
          syncPageImages();
        });
      }

      function applyView() {
        // Transform-only zoom keeps cqh overlay fonts at a stable layout size
        // (resizing image width made text reflow/animate on every wheel tick).
        ensureLayoutStyles();
        grid.style.setProperty('--schematic-canvas-gap', baseGap() + 'px');
        world.style.transform = 'translate3d(' + tx + 'px,' + ty + 'px,0) scale(' + scale + ')';
        if (zoomResetBtn) zoomResetBtn.textContent = Math.round(scale * 100) + '%';
        scheduleSyncPageImages();
      }

      function contentSize() {
        return {
          width: baseWidth * scale,
          height: baseHeight * scale
        };
      }

      function fitToViewport(center) {
        measureBaseSize();
        var rect = viewport.getBoundingClientRect();
        var availW = Math.max(1, rect.width - FIT_PADDING * 2);
        var availH = Math.max(1, rect.height - FIT_PADDING * 2);
        fitScale = clamp(Math.min(availW / baseWidth, availH / baseHeight), MIN_SCALE, MAX_SCALE);
        scale = fitScale;
        if (center !== false) {
          tx = (rect.width - baseWidth * scale) / 2;
          ty = (rect.height - baseHeight * scale) / 2;
        }
        applyView();
      }

      function zoomAt(nextScale, clientX, clientY) {
        var rect = viewport.getBoundingClientRect();
        var x = (clientX == null ? rect.left + rect.width / 2 : clientX) - rect.left;
        var y = (clientY == null ? rect.top + rect.height / 2 : clientY) - rect.top;
        nextScale = clamp(nextScale, MIN_SCALE, MAX_SCALE);
        var worldX = (x - tx) / scale;
        var worldY = (y - ty) / scale;
        scale = nextScale;
        tx = x - worldX * scale;
        ty = y - worldY * scale;
        applyView();
      }

      function pointerDistance(a, b) {
        var dx = a.x - b.x;
        var dy = a.y - b.y;
        return Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
      }

      function endPan() {
        panActive = false;
        panPointerId = null;
        viewport.classList.remove('is-panning');
      }

      function pointerList() {
        var pts = [];
        pointers.forEach(function(p) { pts.push(p); });
        return pts;
      }

      viewport.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(e) {
        // Left-click or middle-click drag to pan (middle also blocks browser autoscroll).
        var isMiddle = e.button === 1;
        if (e.button != null && e.button !== 0 && !isMiddle) return;
        if (e.target.closest('.schematic-canvas-chrome, .schematic-canvas-fs-btn, #schematic-tooltip, #schematic-tooltip-backdrop')) return;

        if (document.activeElement !== viewport) {
          try { viewport.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (err) { viewport.focus(); }
        }

        if (isMiddle) {
          e.preventDefault();
          pressOverlayTarget = null;
        } else {
          // Desktop browsers start a native <a> drag; that blocks canvas panning.
          pressOverlayTarget = e.target.closest(
            '.interactable-schematic-overlay a, .schematic-link a, .interactable-schematic-overlay .schematic-overlay-text'
          );
          if (pressOverlayTarget) {
            if (pressOverlayTarget.tagName === 'A') pressOverlayTarget.setAttribute('draggable', 'false');
            e.preventDefault();
          }
        }

        pointers.set(e.pointerId, { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY });
        try { viewport.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch (err) {}

        if (pointers.size === 2) {
          var pts = pointerList();
          var rect = viewport.getBoundingClientRect();
          var midX = (pts[0].x + pts[1].x) / 2 - rect.left;
          var midY = (pts[0].y + pts[1].y) / 2 - rect.top;
          pinchStartDist = pointerDistance(pts[0], pts[1]);
          pinchStartScale = scale;
          pinchWorldX = (midX - tx) / scale;
          pinchWorldY = (midY - ty) / scale;
          endPan();
          suppressClick = true;
          pressOverlayTarget = null;
          return;
        }

        panActive = true;
        panMoved = false;
        panPointerId = e.pointerId;
        panStartX = e.clientX;
        panStartY = e.clientY;
        panOriginTx = tx;
        panOriginTy = ty;
        viewport.classList.add('is-panning');
      });

      // Middle-click is used for panning — don't open product links in a new tab.
      viewport.addEventListener('auxclick', function(e) {
        if (e.button === 1) e.preventDefault();
      });

      viewport.addEventListener('dragstart', function(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
      });

      viewport.addEventListener('pointermove', function(e) {
        if (!pointers.has(e.pointerId)) return;
        pointers.set(e.pointerId, { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY });

        if (pointers.size === 2) {
          var pts = pointerList();
          var dist = pointerDistance(pts[0], pts[1]);
          if (pinchStartDist > 0) {
            var rect = viewport.getBoundingClientRect();
            var midX = (pts[0].x + pts[1].x) / 2 - rect.left;
            var midY = (pts[0].y + pts[1].y) / 2 - rect.top;
            scale = clamp(pinchStartScale * (dist / pinchStartDist), MIN_SCALE, MAX_SCALE);
            // Keep the original pinch point under the moving fingertip midpoint.
            tx = midX - pinchWorldX * scale;
            ty = midY - pinchWorldY * scale;
            suppressClick = true;
            pressOverlayTarget = null;
            e.preventDefault();
            applyView();
          }
          return;
        }

        if (!panActive || e.pointerId !== panPointerId) return;
        var dx = e.clientX - panStartX;
        var dy = e.clientY - panStartY;
        if (!panMoved && Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) > DRAG_THRESHOLD) {
          panMoved = true;
          suppressClick = true;
          pressOverlayTarget = null;
        }
        if (panMoved) {
          e.preventDefault();
          tx = panOriginTx + dx;
          ty = panOriginTy + dy;
          applyView();
        }
      });

      function onPointerUp(e) {
        if (!pointers.has(e.pointerId)) return;
        pointers.delete(e.pointerId);

        var wasPinching = pinchStartDist > 0;
        var shouldClickOverlay = !panMoved && !wasPinching && !!pressOverlayTarget;
        var overlayTarget = pressOverlayTarget;
        pressOverlayTarget = null;

        if (pointers.size < 2) {
          pinchStartDist = 0;
        }

        if (e.pointerId === panPointerId) {
          endPan();
        }

        // After a pinch, keep dragging with the remaining finger.
        if (wasPinching && pointers.size === 1) {
          var remaining = null;
          pointers.forEach(function(p, id) {
            remaining = { id: id, x: p.x, y: p.y };
          });
          if (remaining) {
            panActive = true;
            panMoved = true;
            panPointerId = remaining.id;
            panStartX = remaining.x;
            panStartY = remaining.y;
            panOriginTx = tx;
            panOriginTy = ty;
            viewport.classList.add('is-panning');
            try { viewport.setPointerCapture(remaining.id); } catch (err) {}
          }
        }

        if (suppressClick) {
          setTimeout(function() { suppressClick = false; }, 0);
        }

        // Pointer capture retargets click to the viewport, so replay a tap on the part label.
        if (shouldClickOverlay && overlayTarget) {
          setTimeout(function() {
            overlayTarget.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent('click', {
              bubbles: true,
              cancelable: true,
              view: window,
              clientX: e.clientX,
              clientY: e.clientY
            }));
          }, 0);
        }
      }

      viewport.addEventListener('pointerup', onPointerUp);
      viewport.addEventListener('pointercancel', onPointerUp);

      viewport.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
        if (!suppressClick) return;
        e.preventDefault();
        e.stopPropagation();
      }, true);

      viewport.addEventListener('wheel', function(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        var factor = e.deltaY < 0 ? ZOOM_STEP : (1 / ZOOM_STEP);
        if (Math.abs(e.deltaY) < 8) factor = e.deltaY < 0 ? 1.05 : 1 / 1.05;
        zoomAt(scale * factor, e.clientX, e.clientY);
      }, { passive: false });

      var lastTapAt = 0;
      var lastTapX = 0;
      var lastTapY = 0;
      viewport.addEventListener('pointerup', function(e) {
        if (e.pointerType !== 'touch' || panMoved || pointers.size > 0) return;
        var now = Date.now();
        var dt = now - lastTapAt;
        var dx = e.clientX - lastTapX;
        var dy = e.clientY - lastTapY;
        if (dt > 0 && dt < 300 && (dx * dx + dy * dy) < 900) {
          if (scale > fitScale * 1.15) fitToViewport(true);
          else zoomAt(Math.min(MAX_SCALE, fitScale * 2.25), e.clientX, e.clientY);
          lastTapAt = 0;
          return;
        }
        lastTapAt = now;
        lastTapX = e.clientX;
        lastTapY = e.clientY;
      });

      if (zoomInBtn) zoomInBtn.addEventListener('click', function() { zoomAt(scale * ZOOM_STEP); });
      if (zoomOutBtn) zoomOutBtn.addEventListener('click', function() { zoomAt(scale / ZOOM_STEP); });
      if (zoomResetBtn) zoomResetBtn.addEventListener('click', function() { fitToViewport(true); });
      if (scrollDownBtn) {
        scrollDownBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
          if (isFullscreen) return;
          var target = stage || root;
          var nextTop = window.scrollY + target.getBoundingClientRect().bottom + 12;
          window.scrollTo({ top: nextTop, behavior: 'smooth' });
        });
      }
      if (fsEnterBtn) fsEnterBtn.addEventListener('click', enterFullscreen);
      fsExitBtns.forEach(function(btn) {
        btn.addEventListener('click', exitFullscreen);
      });

      function onNativeFullscreenChange() {
        var nativeEl = getNativeFullscreenElement();
        if (nativeEl === stage) {
          if (!isFullscreen) {
            isFullscreen = true;
            syncFullscreenUi();
          }
          mountOverlayUi();
          return;
        }
        // Browser exited native fullscreen (often via Escape) — finish exiting ours too.
        if (isFullscreen) {
          isFullscreen = false;
          syncFullscreenUi();
        }
        mountOverlayUi();
      }
      document.addEventListener('fullscreenchange', onNativeFullscreenChange);
      document.addEventListener('webkitfullscreenchange', onNativeFullscreenChange);

      document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
        var tag = e.target && e.target.tagName;
        var typing = tag === 'INPUT' || tag === 'TEXTAREA' || tag === 'SELECT' || (e.target && e.target.isContentEditable);
        if (typing) return;

        if (e.key === 'Escape' && isFullscreen) {
          if (isProductModalOpen()) return;
          e.preventDefault();
          e.stopPropagation();
          exitFullscreen();
          return;
        }

        if ((e.key === 'f' || e.key === 'F') && !e.ctrlKey && !e.metaKey && !e.altKey) {
          if (isProductModalOpen()) return;
          e.preventDefault();
          toggleFullscreen();
        }
      }, true);

      root.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
        if (e.target && (e.target.tagName === 'INPUT' || e.target.tagName === 'TEXTAREA' || e.target.tagName === 'SELECT')) return;
        if (e.key === 'Escape' || e.key === 'f' || e.key === 'F') return;
        if (e.key === '+' || e.key === '=') {
          e.preventDefault();
          zoomAt(scale * ZOOM_STEP);
        } else if (e.key === '-' || e.key === '_') {
          e.preventDefault();
          zoomAt(scale / ZOOM_STEP);
        } else if (e.key === '0') {
          e.preventDefault();
          fitToViewport(true);
        }
      });

      window.addEventListener('resize', function() {
        fitToViewport(true);
      });

      ensureLayoutStyles();
      fitToViewport(true);
      syncPageImages();
    });
  })();

  (function() {
    var tooltip = document.getElementById('schematic-tooltip');
    if (!tooltip) {
      tooltip = document.createElement('div');
      tooltip.id = 'schematic-tooltip';
      document.body.appendChild(tooltip);
    }

    var backdrop = document.getElementById('schematic-tooltip-backdrop');
    if (!backdrop) {
      backdrop = document.createElement('div');
      backdrop.id = 'schematic-tooltip-backdrop';
      document.body.insertBefore(backdrop, tooltip);
    }

    var cartFeedback = document.getElementById('schematic-cart-feedback');
    if (!cartFeedback) {
      cartFeedback = document.createElement('div');
      cartFeedback.id = 'schematic-cart-feedback';
      cartFeedback.className = 'schematic-cart-feedback';
      cartFeedback.setAttribute('aria-live', 'polite');
      cartFeedback.innerHTML = '<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 20 20" fill="currentColor"><path fill-rule="evenodd" d="M10 18a8 8 0 100-16 8 8 0 000 16zm3.857-9.809a.75.75 0 00-1.214-.882l-3.483 4.79-1.88-1.88a.75.75 0 10-1.06 1.061l2.5 2.5a.75.75 0 001.137-.089l4-5.5z" clip-rule="evenodd"/></svg><span>Added to cart</span>';
      document.body.appendChild(cartFeedback);
    }

    var cartFeedbackFail = document.getElementById('schematic-cart-feedback-fail');
    if (!cartFeedbackFail) {
      cartFeedbackFail = document.createElement('div');
      cartFeedbackFail.id = 'schematic-cart-feedback-fail';
      cartFeedbackFail.className = 'schematic-cart-feedback schematic-cart-feedback-fail';
      cartFeedbackFail.setAttribute('aria-live', 'polite');
      cartFeedbackFail.innerHTML = '<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 20 20" fill="currentColor"><path fill-rule="evenodd" d="M18 10a8 8 0 11-16 0 8 8 0 0116 0zm-8-5a.75.75 0 01.75.75v4.5a.75.75 0 01-1.5 0v-4.5A.75.75 0 0110 5zm0 10a1 1 0 100-2 1 1 0 000 2z" clip-rule="evenodd"/></svg><span>Could not add to cart</span>';
      document.body.appendChild(cartFeedbackFail);
    }

    var cartFeedbackViewportHandler = null;

    function applyCartFeedbackViewport(el) {
      if (isMobile() && window.visualViewport) {
        var vv = window.visualViewport;
        var scale = (vv && vv.scale) || 1;
        var w = ((vv && vv.width) || window.innerWidth) * scale;
        var h = ((vv && vv.height) || window.innerHeight) * scale;
        var margin = 32;
        var minSize = 260;
        var left = (vv ? (vv.offsetLeft + (vv.width / 2)) : (window.innerWidth / 2));
        var top = (vv ? (vv.offsetTop + (vv.height / 2)) : (window.innerHeight / 2));
        el.style.left = left + 'px';
        el.style.top = top + 'px';
        el.style.transform = 'translate(-50%, -50%) scale(' + (1 / scale) + ')';
        el.style.maxWidth = Math.max(w - margin, minSize) + 'px';
        el.style.maxHeight = Math.max(h - margin, minSize) + 'px';
      } else {
        el.style.left = '';
        el.style.top = '';
        el.style.transform = '';
        el.style.maxWidth = '';
        el.style.maxHeight = '';
      }
    }

    function showCartFeedbackToast(el, duration) {
      duration = duration || 1800;
      cartFeedback.classList.remove('schematic-cart-feedback-visible');
      cartFeedbackFail.classList.remove('schematic-cart-feedback-visible');
      applyCartFeedbackViewport(el);
      if (isMobile() && window.visualViewport && !cartFeedbackViewportHandler) {
        cartFeedbackViewportHandler = function() {
          applyCartFeedbackViewport(cartFeedback);
          applyCartFeedbackViewport(cartFeedbackFail);
        };
        window.visualViewport.addEventListener('resize', cartFeedbackViewportHandler);
      }
      el.classList.add('schematic-cart-feedback-visible');
      clearTimeout(showCartFeedbackToast._timeout);
      showCartFeedbackToast._timeout = setTimeout(function() {
        el.classList.remove('schematic-cart-feedback-visible');
        if (cartFeedbackViewportHandler && window.visualViewport) {
          window.visualViewport.removeEventListener('resize', cartFeedbackViewportHandler);
          cartFeedbackViewportHandler = null;
        }
      }, duration);
    }

    function showCartFeedback(quantity) {
      var span = cartFeedback.querySelector('span');
      if (span) {
        span.textContent = 'Added ' + (quantity || 1) + ' to cart';
      }
      showCartFeedbackToast(cartFeedback);
    }

    function showCartFeedbackFail(msg) {
      if (msg) cartFeedbackFail.querySelector('span').textContent = msg;
      else cartFeedbackFail.querySelector('span').textContent = 'Could not add to cart';
      showCartFeedbackToast(cartFeedbackFail);
    }

    function isMobile() {
      return window.matchMedia('(max-width: 768px)').matches || ('ontouchstart' in window && window.innerWidth < 1024);
    }

    // iOS 18+ Safari workaround: trigger light haptic using <input type="checkbox" switch>.
    // If unsupported, fall back to Vibration API (similar to web-haptics / ios-haptics wrappers).
    function isIosSafariFamily() {
      var ua = navigator.userAgent || '';
      var isIOS = /iP(hone|ad|od)/.test(ua);
      if (!isIOS) return false;
      return !/CriOS|FxiOS|EdgiOS|OPiOS/i.test(ua);
    }

    var iosSwitchHapticNode = null;

    function ensureIosSwitchHapticNode() {
      if (iosSwitchHapticNode) return iosSwitchHapticNode;
      if (!isIosSafariFamily()) return null;
      var wrap = document.createElement('div');
      wrap.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
      wrap.style.position = 'fixed';
      wrap.style.left = '-9999px';
      wrap.style.top = '-9999px';
      wrap.style.opacity = '0';
      wrap.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
      var input = document.createElement('input');
      input.type = 'checkbox';
      input.setAttribute('switch', '');
      input.id = 'schematic-ios-switch-haptic';
      var label = document.createElement('label');
      label.setAttribute('for', input.id);
      wrap.appendChild(input);
      wrap.appendChild(label);
      document.body.appendChild(wrap);
      iosSwitchHapticNode = { wrap: wrap, input: input, label: label };
      return iosSwitchHapticNode;
    }

    function triggerTapHaptic() {
      var helper = ensureIosSwitchHapticNode();
      if (helper) {
        helper.input.checked = !helper.input.checked;
        helper.label.click();
        return;
      }
      if (navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate(10);
    }

    function triggerSpinnerChangeHaptic(spinner) {
      if (!spinner) return;
      var now = Date.now();
      var last = spinner._hapticLastAt || 0;
      if (now - last < 100) return; // cap at 10 haptics/sec while spinning
      spinner._hapticLastAt = now;
      triggerTapHaptic();
    }

    function getHandleFromHref(href) {
      var m = (href || '').match(/\/products\/([^\/\?#]+)/);
      return m ? m[1] : null;
    }

    var SCHEMATIC_TOOLTIP_IMG_WIDTH = 280;
    var SCHEMATIC_UNAVAILABLE_PHONE_HREF = "";
    var SCHEMATIC_UNAVAILABLE_PHONE_LABEL = "";
    var SCHEMATIC_UNAVAILABLE_EMAIL_HREF = "";
    var SCHEMATIC_UNAVAILABLE_EMAIL_LABEL = "";

    function schematicShopifySizedImageUrl(url, width) {
      if (!url || !width) return url || '';
      var base = String(url).split('?')[0];
      return base + '?width=' + width;
    }

    function formatPriceWithDecimals(priceInCents, fallbackFormattedPrice) {
      if (priceInCents == null || priceInCents === '') return fallbackFormattedPrice || '';

      if (typeof window.Shopify !== 'undefined' && window.Shopify.formatMoney) {
        return window.Shopify.formatMoney(priceInCents, '${{ amount }}');
      }

      var numericPrice = Number(priceInCents);
      if (isNaN(numericPrice)) return fallbackFormattedPrice || '';
      return '$' + (numericPrice / 100).toFixed(2);
    }

    function formatVariantPriceRange(variants, fallbackFormatted) {
      if (!variants || variants.length <= 1) return '';
      var prices = [];
      for (var i = 0; i < variants.length; i++) {
        var p = variants[i] && variants[i].price;
        if (p != null && p !== '' && !isNaN(Number(p))) prices.push(Number(p));
      }
      if (prices.length === 0) return '';
      var min = Math.min.apply(null, prices);
      var max = Math.max.apply(null, prices);
      var low = formatPriceWithDecimals(min, fallbackFormatted);
      var high = formatPriceWithDecimals(max, fallbackFormatted);
      if (min === max) return low;
      return low + ' - ' + high;
    }

    function escapeOptionLabel(s) {
      return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;').replace(/"/g, '&quot;');
    }

    function normalizeVariant(v) {
      if (!v || typeof v !== 'object') return null;
      return {
        id: v.id,
        title: v.title,
        option1: v.option1,
        option2: v.option2,
        option3: v.option3,
        available: v.available,
        price: v.price != null ? v.price : null
      };
    }

    function firstAvailableVariant(variants) {
      if (!variants || variants.length === 0) return null;
      var v = variants[0];
      var available;
      if (variants.length > 0) {
        available = false;
        for (var i = 0; i < variants.length; i++) {
          if (variants[i].available === true) { v = variants[i]; available = true; break; }
        }
        if (!available) {
          var anyTrue = false, allFalse = true;
          for (var j = 0; j < variants.length; j++) {
            var va = variants[j].available;
            if (va === true) anyTrue = true;
            if (va !== false) allFalse = false;
          }
          available = anyTrue ? true : (allFalse ? false : true);
        }
      }
      if (!v && variants[0]) v = variants[0];
      return v;
    }

    function getVariantById(product, variantId) {
      var list = product && product.variants;
      if (!list || variantId == null) return null;
      for (var i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
        if (list[i].id == variantId) return list[i];
      }
      return null;
    }

    function pickModalDefaultVariantId(cacheEntry) {
      if (!cacheEntry || cacheEntry.notFound) return null;
      var variants = cacheEntry.variants;
      if (!variants || variants.length <= 1) return cacheEntry.variantId;
      var v = firstAvailableVariant(variants);
      return v && v.id != null ? v.id : (variants[0] && variants[0].id);
    }

    function variantOptionLabel(v) {
      if (!v) return '';
      if (v.title) return v.title;
      var parts = [v.option1, v.option2, v.option3].filter(function(x) { return x != null && String(x).trim() !== ''; });
      return parts.join(' / ');
    }

    function variantPickerLabelText(product) {
      var opts = product && product.options;
      if (opts && opts.length) {
        var names = [];
        for (var i = 0; i < opts.length; i++) {
          var o = opts[i];
          if (typeof o === 'string' && o.trim()) names.push(o.trim());
          else if (o && typeof o.name === 'string' && o.name.trim()) names.push(o.name.trim());
        }
        if (names.length) return names.join(' · ');
      }
      return 'Choose an option';
    }

    function buildVariantPickerHtml(product, selectedVariantId) {
      var variants = product && product.variants;
      if (!variants || variants.length <= 1) return '';
      var effectiveId = selectedVariantId;
      var vCur = getVariantById(product, selectedVariantId);
      if (!vCur || vCur.available === false) {
        var fa = firstAvailableVariant(variants);
        effectiveId = fa && fa.id != null ? fa.id : selectedVariantId;
      }
      var labelText = variantPickerLabelText(product);
      var html = '<div class="tooltip-variant-row"><label class="tooltip-variant-label" for="schematic-variant-select">' + escapeOptionLabel(labelText) + '</label>' +
        '<div class="tooltip-variant-picker-align">' +
        '<div class="tooltip-variant-select-wrap">' +
        '<select id="schematic-variant-select" class="tooltip-variant-select" aria-label="' + escapeOptionLabel(labelText) + '">';
      for (var i = 0; i < variants.length; i++) {
        var v = variants[i];
        if (!v || v.id == null) continue;
        var optLabel = variantOptionLabel(v);
        var dis = v.available === false ? ' disabled' : '';
        var sel = (effectiveId != null && String(v.id) === String(effectiveId)) ? ' selected' : '';
        var suffix = v.available === false ? ' — Sold out' : '';
        html += '<option value="' + v.id + '"' + sel + dis +
          ' data-price="' + (v.price != null ? String(v.price).replace(/"/g, '&quot;') : '') + '"' +
          ' data-available="' + (v.available !== false ? '1' : '0') + '">' +
          escapeOptionLabel(optLabel || ('#' + v.id)) + escapeOptionLabel(suffix) + '</option>';
      }
      html += '</select></div><div class="tooltip-variant-picker-spacer" aria-hidden="true"></div></div></div>';
      return html;
    }

    function syncModalVariantUi(root) {
      root = root || tooltip;
      var sel = root.querySelector('.tooltip-variant-select');
      if (!sel) return;
      var opt = sel.options[sel.selectedIndex];
      if (!opt) return;
      var addBtn = root.querySelector('.tooltip-btn-add');
      var available = opt.getAttribute('data-available') === '1';
      if (addBtn) {
        addBtn.setAttribute('data-variant-id', sel.value);
        addBtn.disabled = !available;
      }
    }

    function resolveModalVariantForDisplay(product, preferredId) {
      var variants = product && product.variants;
      if (!variants || variants.length === 0) return null;
      if (variants.length === 1) return variants[0];
      var v = getVariantById(product, preferredId);
      if (v && v.available !== false) return v;
      return firstAvailableVariant(variants) || variants[0];
    }

    function buildTooltipHtml(product, fallbackTitle, opts) {
      opts = opts || {};
      var title = (product && product.title) || fallbackTitle;
      var imgUrl = product && product.imgUrl;
      var thumbUrl = product && product.thumbUrl;
      var isModalUi = !!(opts.isMobile || opts.isModal);
      var modalVariant = isModalUi ? resolveModalVariantForDisplay(product, opts.variantId) : null;
      var variants = product && product.variants;
      var multiVariant = variants && variants.length > 1;
      var price = '';
      if (product) {
        if (multiVariant) {
          price = formatVariantPriceRange(variants, product.priceFormatted) ||
            formatPriceWithDecimals(product.price, product.priceFormatted);
        } else if (isModalUi && modalVariant && modalVariant.price != null) {
          price = formatPriceWithDecimals(modalVariant.price, product.priceFormatted);
        } else {
          price = formatPriceWithDecimals(product.price, product.priceFormatted);
        }
      }
      var description = product && product.description;
      var escapedTitle = (title || '').replace(/"/g, '&quot;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;');
      var titleId = isModalUi ? ' id="schematic-modal-title"' : '';
      var imgHtml = '';
      if (imgUrl) {
        var closeBtn = isModalUi ? '<button type="button" class="tooltip-close" aria-label="Close">&times;</button>' : '';
        imgHtml = '<div class="tooltip-img-wrapper">' + closeBtn +
          (thumbUrl ? '<div class="tooltip-img-placeholder" style="background-image:url(\'' + thumbUrl.replace(/'/g, '\\\'') + '\')" aria-hidden="true"></div>' : '') +
          '<img src="' + imgUrl + '" alt="' + escapedTitle + '" class="tooltip-img" loading="lazy" onload="this.classList.add(\'loaded\')" onerror="this.classList.add(\'loaded\')">' +
          '</div>';
      }
      var variantPickerHtml = '';
      if (isModalUi && product && product.variants && product.variants.length > 1 && opts.available !== false) {
        variantPickerHtml = buildVariantPickerHtml(product, opts.variantId);
      }
      var hint = isModalUi ? '' : '<div class="tooltip-hint">Click for details | Shift + click: quick add (single variant) or choose variant</div>';
      var actions = '';
      if (isModalUi && opts.productUrl) {
        var addRow = '';
        if (opts.available === false) {
          addRow = '<div class="tooltip-add-row tooltip-out-of-stock-row"><span class="tooltip-out-of-stock">Out of stock</span></div>';
        } else if (opts.variantId || (modalVariant && modalVariant.id != null)) {
          var addVid = modalVariant && modalVariant.id != null ? modalVariant.id : opts.variantId;
          var addDisabled = modalVariant && modalVariant.available === false ? ' disabled' : '';
          addRow = '<div class="tooltip-add-row">' +
            '<button type="button" class="tooltip-btn tooltip-btn-add" data-variant-id="' + addVid + '"' + addDisabled + '>Add to cart</button>' +
            '<div class="tooltip-qty-stepper">' +
            '<div class="tooltip-qty-spinner-zone">' +
            '<div class="tooltip-qty-spinner" role="spinbutton" aria-label="Quantity" aria-valuenow="1" aria-valuemin="1" aria-valuemax="999" data-value="1" tabindex="0">' +
            '<span class="tooltip-qty-arrow tooltip-qty-arrow-up" aria-hidden="true"></span>' +
            '<div class="tooltip-qty-values">' +
            '<span class="tooltip-qty-prev" aria-hidden="true"></span>' +
            '<span class="tooltip-qty-value" data-value="1">1</span>' +
            '<span class="tooltip-qty-next" aria-hidden="true">2</span>' +
            '</div>' +
            '<span class="tooltip-qty-arrow tooltip-qty-arrow-down" aria-hidden="true"></span>' +
            '</div>' +
            '</div>' +
            '</div>' +
            '</div>';
        }
        actions = '<div class="tooltip-actions">' +
          addRow +
          '<div class="tooltip-listing-row">' +
          '<a href="' + (opts.productUrl || '').replace(/"/g, '&quot;') + '" class="tooltip-btn tooltip-btn-view">View listing</a>' +
          '<button type="button" class="tooltip-btn tooltip-btn-close" data-tooltip-dismiss>Close</button>' +
          '</div>' +
          '</div>';
      }
      var descHtml = '';
      if (isModalUi && description) {
        descHtml = '<div class="tooltip-description">' + description + '</div>';
      }
      var priceHtml = '';
      if (price) {
        priceHtml = '<div class="tooltip-price">' + String(price).replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;') + '</div>';
      }
      return (isModalUi && !imgUrl ? '<button type="button" class="tooltip-close" aria-label="Close">&times;</button>' : '') +
        imgHtml +
        '<div class="tooltip-title"' + titleId + '>' + escapedTitle + '</div>' +
        priceHtml +
        variantPickerHtml +
        hint + actions + descHtml;
    }

    function setTooltipContent(html) {
      tooltip.innerHTML = html;
      var img = tooltip.querySelector('.tooltip-img');
      if (img && img.complete) img.classList.add('loaded');
      if (tooltip.querySelector('.tooltip-variant-select')) syncModalVariantUi(tooltip);
    }

    var productCache = {};

    var imagePreloadQueue = [];
    var imagePreloadSeen = {};
    var imagePreloadScheduled = false;
    var IMAGE_PRELOAD_BATCH = 2;

    function queueImageForPreload(url) {
      if (!url || imagePreloadSeen[url]) return;
      imagePreloadSeen[url] = true;
      imagePreloadQueue.push(url);
      scheduleImagePreload();
    }

    function scheduleImagePreload() {
      if (imagePreloadScheduled || !imagePreloadQueue.length) return;
      imagePreloadScheduled = true;
      var whenIdle = window.requestIdleCallback || function(fn) { setTimeout(fn, 1); };
      whenIdle(function() {
        imagePreloadScheduled = false;
        processImagePreloadQueue();
      }, { timeout: 1500 });
    }

    function processImagePreloadQueue() {
      var n = Math.min(IMAGE_PRELOAD_BATCH, imagePreloadQueue.length);
      for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        var url = imagePreloadQueue.shift();
        if (!url) continue;
        var im = new Image();
        if ('fetchPriority' in im) im.fetchPriority = 'low';
        im.src = url;
      }
      if (imagePreloadQueue.length) scheduleImagePreload();
    }

    function canvasPageCount() {
      var max = 0;
      document.querySelectorAll('[data-schematic-canvas]').forEach(function(el) {
        var n = Number(el.getAttribute('data-page-count')) || 0;
        if (n > max) max = n;
      });
      return max;
    }

    // Always evaluate product links (JSON). Defer product *images* until tooltip hover/tap
    // on mobile or large canvases so we don't decode hundreds of thumbnails up front.
    function shouldDeferProductImages() {
      return isMobile() || canvasPageCount() >= 8;
    }

    (function loadPreloadedProducts() {
      document.querySelectorAll('.interactable-schematic-overlay[data-product-preload]').forEach(function(el) {
        var raw = el.getAttribute('data-product-preload');
        el.removeAttribute('data-product-preload');
        if (!raw) return;
        try {
          var data = JSON.parse(raw);
          for (var h in data) if (data.hasOwnProperty(h)) productCache[h] = data[h];
        } catch (e) {}
      });
      if (shouldDeferProductImages()) return;
      for (var h in productCache) if (productCache.hasOwnProperty(h) && productCache[h].imgUrl) {
        queueImageForPreload(productCache[h].imgUrl);
      }
    })();

    function getUnavailablePartTitle(el) {
      if (!el) return 'Part not available online';
      var handle = (el.getAttribute('data-product-handle') || '').trim();
      if (handle) return handle;
      var fromHref = getHandleFromHref(el.getAttribute('href') || '');
      if (fromHref) return fromHref;
      return ((el.getAttribute('title') || el.textContent) || '').trim() || 'Part not available online';
    }

    function replaceLinkWithText(link) {
      var span = document.createElement('span');
      span.className = 'schematic-overlay-text';
      if (link.getAttribute('style')) span.setAttribute('style', link.getAttribute('style'));
      var handle = getHandleFromHref(link.getAttribute('href') || '');
      if (handle) span.setAttribute('data-product-handle', handle);
      span.textContent = link.textContent || link.getAttribute('title') || '';
      link.parentNode.replaceChild(span, link);
      bindUnavailablePartEvents(span);
    }

    function replaceLinksForHandle(handle) {
      if (hoveredHandle === handle) {
        tooltip.style.display = 'none';
        tooltip.innerHTML = '';
        hoveredHandle = null;
      }
      document.querySelectorAll('.interactable-schematic-overlay a[href*="/products/"]').forEach(function(link) {
        if (getHandleFromHref(link.getAttribute('href')) === handle) {
          replaceLinkWithText(link);
        }
      });
    }

    function preloadProduct(handle, callback) {
      if (productCache[handle] !== undefined) {
        if (callback) callback();
        return;
      }
      var productUrl = (typeof window.Shopify !== 'undefined' && window.Shopify.routes && window.Shopify.routes.root)
        ? (window.Shopify.routes.root + 'products/' + encodeURIComponent(handle) + '.js')
        : ('/products/' + encodeURIComponent(handle) + '.js');
      fetch(productUrl)
        .then(function(r) {
          if (!r.ok) {
            productCache[handle] = { notFound: true };
            replaceLinksForHandle(handle);
            return;
          }
          return r.json();
        })
        .then(function(data) {
          if (!data) return;
          var product = data.product || data;
          var img = product.featured_image || (product.images && product.images[0]);
          var rawImgUrl = img ? (typeof img === 'string' ? img : (img.src || img.url)) : '';
          var imgUrl = rawImgUrl ? schematicShopifySizedImageUrl(rawImgUrl, SCHEMATIC_TOOLTIP_IMG_WIDTH) : '';
          var thumbUrl = rawImgUrl ? schematicShopifySizedImageUrl(rawImgUrl, 40) : '';
          var variants = product.variants || [];
          var v = variants[0];
          var available;
          if (typeof product.available === 'boolean') {
            available = product.available;
          } else if (variants.length > 0) {
            available = false;
            for (var i = 0; i < variants.length; i++) {
              if (variants[i].available === true) { v = variants[i]; available = true; break; }
            }
            if (!available) {
              var anyTrue = false, allFalse = true;
              for (var j = 0; j < variants.length; j++) {
                var va = variants[j].available;
                if (va === true) anyTrue = true;
                if (va !== false) allFalse = false;
              }
              available = anyTrue ? true : (allFalse ? false : true);
            }
          } else {
            available = true;
          }
          if (!v && variants[0]) v = variants[0];
          var variantId = v && v.id;
          var price = v && v.price != null ? v.price : null;
          var priceFormatted = formatPriceWithDecimals(price, '');
          var description = (product.body_html || product.description || product.body || '').trim();
          var optionsList = product.options || [];
          var normalizedVariants = [];
          for (var vi = 0; vi < variants.length; vi++) {
            var nv = normalizeVariant(variants[vi]);
            if (nv) normalizedVariants.push(nv);
          }
          productCache[handle] = {
            title: product.title || handle,
            imgUrl: imgUrl,
            thumbUrl: thumbUrl,
            price: price,
            priceFormatted: priceFormatted,
            variantId: variantId,
            available: available,
            description: description,
            options: optionsList,
            variants: normalizedVariants
          };
          // Product images load with the tooltip; only warm the image cache on small canvases.
          if (imgUrl && !shouldDeferProductImages()) queueImageForPreload(imgUrl);
        })
        .catch(function() {
          productCache[handle] = { notFound: true };
          replaceLinksForHandle(handle);
        })
        .finally(function() {
          if (callback) callback();
        });
    }

    function preloadAllInParallel(handles) {
      handles.forEach(function(handle) {
        preloadProduct(handle);
      });
    }

    var handles = [];
    document.querySelectorAll('.interactable-schematic-overlay a[href*="/products/"]').forEach(function(link) {
      var h = getHandleFromHref(link.getAttribute('href'));
      if (h && handles.indexOf(h) === -1) handles.push(h);
    });

    // Always crawl product JSON so missing parts convert to text; images stay hover/tap-only when deferred.
    if (handles.length) {
      var whenIdle = window.requestIdleCallback || function(fn) { setTimeout(fn, 1); };
      whenIdle(function() {
        preloadAllInParallel(handles);
      }, { timeout: 2000 });
    }

    var hoveredHandle = null;
    var hoveredUnavailableEl = null;

    function buildUnavailablePartTooltipHtml(partTitle) {
      var escapedTitle = (partTitle || 'Part not available online').replace(/"/g, '&quot;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;');
      return '<div class="tooltip-title">' + escapedTitle + '</div>' +
        '<p class="tooltip-unavailable-message">Not available online. Order by phone or email.</p>' +
        '<div class="tooltip-hint">Click for contact options</div>';
    }

    function bindUnavailablePartEvents(span) {
      if (!span || span._schematicUnavailableBound) return;
      span._schematicUnavailableBound = true;

      span.addEventListener('mouseenter', function() {
        if (isMobile()) return;
        hoveredUnavailableEl = span;
        hoveredHandle = null;
        setTooltipContent(buildUnavailablePartTooltipHtml(getUnavailablePartTitle(span)));
        tooltip.classList.remove('tooltip-mobile');
        tooltip.style.display = 'block';
      });

      span.addEventListener('mousemove', function(e) {
        if (isMobile()) return;
        var offset = 24;
        var margin = 8;
        var tooltipW = tooltip.offsetWidth || 288;
        var tooltipH = tooltip.offsetHeight || 300;
        var x = Math.max(margin, Math.min(e.clientX + offset, window.innerWidth - tooltipW - margin));
        var y = Math.max(margin, Math.min(e.clientY - tooltipH / 2, window.innerHeight - tooltipH - margin));
        tooltip.style.left = x + 'px';
        tooltip.style.top = y + 'px';
      });

      span.addEventListener('mouseleave', function() {
        if (isMobile()) return;
        if (tooltip.classList.contains('tooltip-mobile')) return;
        if (hoveredUnavailableEl === span) hoveredUnavailableEl = null;
        tooltip.style.display = 'none';
        tooltip.innerHTML = '';
      });

      span.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        triggerTapHaptic();
        openUnavailablePartModal(span);
      });
    }

    function buildUnavailablePartModalHtml(partTitle) {
      var title = partTitle || 'Part not available online';
      var escapedTitle = title.replace(/"/g, '&quot;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;');
      var actions = '';
      var phoneHref = (SCHEMATIC_UNAVAILABLE_PHONE_HREF || '').trim();
      var emailHref = (SCHEMATIC_UNAVAILABLE_EMAIL_HREF || '').trim();
      if (phoneHref || emailHref) {
        actions = '<div class="tooltip-unavailable-actions">';
        if (phoneHref) {
          actions += '<a href="' + escapeOptionLabel(phoneHref) + '" class="tooltip-btn tooltip-btn-contact tooltip-btn-phone">' +
            escapeOptionLabel(SCHEMATIC_UNAVAILABLE_PHONE_LABEL || phoneHref) + '</a>';
        }
        if (emailHref) {
          actions += '<a href="' + escapeOptionLabel(emailHref) + '" class="tooltip-btn tooltip-btn-contact tooltip-btn-email">' +
            escapeOptionLabel(SCHEMATIC_UNAVAILABLE_EMAIL_LABEL || emailHref) + '</a>';
        }
        actions += '</div>';
      }
      return '<button type="button" class="tooltip-close" aria-label="Close">&times;</button>' +
        '<div class="tooltip-title" id="schematic-modal-title">' + escapedTitle + '</div>' +
        '<p class="tooltip-unavailable-message">Parts not available online can still be ordered by phone or email.</p>' +
        actions;
    }

    function openUnavailablePartModal(el) {
      modalFocusRestore = el;
      setTooltipContent(buildUnavailablePartModalHtml(getUnavailablePartTitle(el)));
      tooltip.classList.add('tooltip-mobile');
      tooltip.style.display = 'block';
      tooltip.style.left = '';
      tooltip.style.top = '';
      backdrop.style.display = 'block';
      openMobileModal();
    }

    function openSchematicProductModal(link, handle) {
      modalFocusRestore = link;
      var href = link.getAttribute('href') || '';
      var fallbackTitle = handle;
      var showAsModal = isMobile();

      function showModalTooltip() {
        var c = productCache[handle];
        if (c && c.notFound) {
          setTooltipContent(buildUnavailablePartModalHtml(handle));
          tooltip.classList.add('tooltip-mobile');
          tooltip.style.display = 'block';
          tooltip.style.left = '';
          tooltip.style.top = '';
          backdrop.style.display = 'block';
          openMobileModal();
          return;
        }
        var available = c && !c.notFound && c.available !== false;
        var modalVid = c && !c.notFound ? pickModalDefaultVariantId(c) : null;
        var opts = {
          isMobile: showAsModal,
          isModal: true,
          productUrl: href,
          variantId: modalVid && available ? modalVid : null,
          available: c && !c.notFound ? c.available !== false : undefined
        };
        setTooltipContent(buildTooltipHtml(c, fallbackTitle, opts));
        tooltip.classList.add('tooltip-mobile');
        tooltip.style.display = 'block';
        tooltip.style.left = '';
        tooltip.style.top = '';
        backdrop.style.display = 'block';
        openMobileModal();
      }

      var cached = productCache[handle];
      if (cached) {
        showModalTooltip();
        return;
      }

      tooltip.innerHTML = '<span class="tooltip-title">Loading…</span>';
      tooltip.classList.add('tooltip-mobile');
      tooltip.style.display = 'block';
      tooltip.style.left = '';
      tooltip.style.top = '';
      backdrop.style.display = 'block';
      openMobileModal();

      preloadProduct(handle, function() {
        showModalTooltip();
      });
    }

    document.querySelectorAll('.interactable-schematic-overlay a[href*="/products/"]').forEach(function(link) {
      var handle = getHandleFromHref(link.getAttribute('href'));
      if (!handle) return;

      link.addEventListener('mouseenter', function() {
        if (isMobile()) return;
        hoveredHandle = handle;
        var fallbackTitle = handle;
        var cached = productCache[handle];

        if (cached) {
          if (cached.notFound) {
            setTooltipContent(buildUnavailablePartTooltipHtml(handle));
          } else {
            setTooltipContent(buildTooltipHtml(cached, fallbackTitle));
          }
          tooltip.style.display = 'block';
          return;
        }

        tooltip.innerHTML = '<span class="tooltip-title">Loading…</span>';
        tooltip.style.display = 'block';

        preloadProduct(handle, function() {
          if (hoveredHandle === handle) {
            var loaded = productCache[handle];
            if (loaded && loaded.notFound) {
              setTooltipContent(buildUnavailablePartTooltipHtml(handle));
            } else {
              setTooltipContent(buildTooltipHtml(loaded, fallbackTitle));
            }
          }
        });
      });

      link.addEventListener('mousemove', function(e) {
        if (isMobile()) return;
        var offset = 24;
        var margin = 8;
        var tooltipW = tooltip.offsetWidth || 288;
        var tooltipH = tooltip.offsetHeight || 300;
        var x = Math.max(margin, Math.min(e.clientX + offset, window.innerWidth - tooltipW - margin));
        var y = Math.max(margin, Math.min(e.clientY - tooltipH / 2, window.innerHeight - tooltipH - margin));
        tooltip.style.left = x + 'px';
        tooltip.style.top = y + 'px';
      });

      link.addEventListener('mouseleave', function() {
        if (isMobile()) return;
        if (tooltip.classList.contains('tooltip-mobile')) return;
        hoveredHandle = null;
        tooltip.style.display = 'none';
        tooltip.innerHTML = '';
      });

      link.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
        triggerTapHaptic();
        if (e.shiftKey) {
          e.preventDefault();
          function hasMultipleVariants(c) {
            return c && !c.notFound && c.variants && c.variants.length > 1;
          }
          function variantIdForQuickAdd(c) {
            if (!c || c.notFound) return null;
            return c.variantId;
          }
          var cached = productCache[handle];
          if (hasMultipleVariants(cached)) {
            openSchematicProductModal(link, handle);
            return;
          }
          var variantId = variantIdForQuickAdd(cached);
          var available = cached && cached.available !== false;
          if (!variantId || !available) {
            preloadProduct(handle, function() {
              var c = productCache[handle];
              if (hasMultipleVariants(c)) {
                openSchematicProductModal(link, handle);
                return;
              }
              var vid = variantIdForQuickAdd(c);
              if (vid && !c.notFound && c.available !== false) quickAddToCart(vid);
              else if (typeof window.alert === 'function') window.alert(c && c.available === false ? 'This item is out of stock.' : 'Product unavailable.');
            });
          } else {
            quickAddToCart(variantId);
          }
          return;
        }

        e.preventDefault();
        openSchematicProductModal(link, handle);
      });
    });

    document.querySelectorAll('.schematic-overlay-text').forEach(bindUnavailablePartEvents);

    var visualViewportResizeHandler = null;
    var scrollCloseHandler = null;
    var viewportScrollCloseHandler = null;
    var modalKeyboardHandler = null;
    var modalFocusRestore = null;

    function getModalFocusables() {
      if (!tooltip || !tooltip.classList.contains('tooltip-mobile')) return [];
      var selector = 'button:not([disabled]), a[href], input:not([disabled]), select:not([disabled]), textarea:not([disabled]), [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])';
      var list = Array.prototype.slice.call(tooltip.querySelectorAll(selector)).filter(function(el) {
        return el.offsetParent !== null && (el.offsetWidth > 0 || el.offsetHeight > 0);
      });
      if (list.length === 0 && tooltip.getAttribute('tabindex') === '-1') list = [tooltip];
      return list;
    }

    function handleModalKeyboard(e) {
      if (!tooltip.classList.contains('tooltip-mobile') || tooltip.style.display !== 'block') return;
      if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        e.preventDefault();
        closeMobileTooltip();
        return;
      }
      if (e.key !== 'Tab') return;
      var focusables = getModalFocusables();
      if (focusables.length === 0) return;
      var first = focusables[0];
      var last = focusables[focusables.length - 1];
      var active = document.activeElement;
      if (!tooltip.contains(active)) {
        e.preventDefault();
        first.focus();
        return;
      }
      if (e.shiftKey) {
        if (active === first) {
          e.preventDefault();
          last.focus();
        }
      } else {
        if (active === last) {
          e.preventDefault();
          first.focus();
        }
      }
    }

    function applyModalViewportSize() {
      var vv = window.visualViewport;
      var scale = (vv && vv.scale) || 1;
      var w = ((vv && vv.width) || window.innerWidth) * scale;
      var h = ((vv && vv.height) || window.innerHeight) * scale;
      var margin = 32;
      var left = (vv ? (vv.offsetLeft + (vv.width / 2)) : (window.innerWidth / 2));
      var top = (vv ? (vv.offsetTop + (vv.height / 2)) : (window.innerHeight / 2));

      tooltip.style.left = left + 'px';
      tooltip.style.top = top + 'px';
      tooltip.style.transform = 'translate(-50%, -50%) scale(' + (1 / scale) + ')';
      tooltip.style.maxWidth = (w - margin) + 'px';
      tooltip.style.maxHeight = (h - margin) + 'px';
    }

    function closeMobileTooltip() {
      tooltip.classList.remove('schematic-spinner-dragging');
      document.body.classList.remove('schematic-spinner-dragging');
      if (tooltip._clearSpinnerCollapse) tooltip._clearSpinnerCollapse();
      if (visualViewportResizeHandler && window.visualViewport) {
        window.visualViewport.removeEventListener('resize', visualViewportResizeHandler);
        visualViewportResizeHandler = null;
      }
      if (scrollCloseHandler) {
        window.removeEventListener('scroll', scrollCloseHandler, true);
        scrollCloseHandler = null;
      }
      if (viewportScrollCloseHandler && window.visualViewport) {
        window.visualViewport.removeEventListener('scroll', viewportScrollCloseHandler);
        viewportScrollCloseHandler = null;
      }
      if (modalKeyboardHandler) {
        document.removeEventListener('keydown', modalKeyboardHandler, true);
        modalKeyboardHandler = null;
      }
      tooltip.removeAttribute('role');
      tooltip.removeAttribute('aria-modal');
      tooltip.removeAttribute('aria-labelledby');
      if (modalFocusRestore && typeof modalFocusRestore.focus === 'function') {
        modalFocusRestore.focus();
        modalFocusRestore = null;
      }
      tooltip.classList.remove('tooltip-mobile');
      tooltip.style.display = 'none';
      tooltip.style.left = '';
      tooltip.style.top = '';
      tooltip.style.transform = '';
      tooltip.style.maxWidth = '';
      tooltip.style.maxHeight = '';
      tooltip.innerHTML = '';
      backdrop.style.display = 'none';
    }

    function openMobileModal() {
      tooltip.setAttribute('role', 'dialog');
      tooltip.setAttribute('aria-modal', 'true');
      var titleEl = tooltip.querySelector('.tooltip-title[id="schematic-modal-title"]');
      if (titleEl) tooltip.setAttribute('aria-labelledby', 'schematic-modal-title');
      tooltip.setAttribute('tabindex', '-1');
      if (!modalKeyboardHandler) {
        modalKeyboardHandler = handleModalKeyboard;
        document.addEventListener('keydown', modalKeyboardHandler, true);
      }
      applyModalViewportSize();
      if (window.visualViewport && !visualViewportResizeHandler) {
        visualViewportResizeHandler = function() { applyModalViewportSize(); };
        window.visualViewport.addEventListener('resize', visualViewportResizeHandler);
      }
      if (!scrollCloseHandler) {
        scrollCloseHandler = function(e) {
          if (tooltip.classList.contains('schematic-spinner-dragging')) return;
          var t = e.target;
          if (!t || typeof t.closest !== 'function' || !t.closest('#schematic-tooltip')) closeMobileTooltip();
        };
        window.addEventListener('scroll', scrollCloseHandler, true);
      }
      if (window.visualViewport && !viewportScrollCloseHandler) {
        viewportScrollCloseHandler = function() {
          if (tooltip.classList.contains('schematic-spinner-dragging')) return;
          closeMobileTooltip();
        };
        window.visualViewport.addEventListener('scroll', viewportScrollCloseHandler);
      }
      requestAnimationFrame(function() {
        var focusables = getModalFocusables();
        if (focusables.length > 0) focusables[0].focus();
        else tooltip.focus();
      });
    }

    backdrop.addEventListener('click', closeMobileTooltip);

    (function setupQtySpinner() {
      var PX_PER_STEP = 8;
      var spinStartY, spinStartVal, spinStartX, spinPtrId;
      var spinVelHistory = [];
      var spinMoveHandler, spinUpHandler;
      var CLICK_THRESHOLD = 6;
      var COLLAPSE_DELAY = 2000;
      var collapseTimer = null;

      function expandSpinner(spinner, noCollapseTimer) {
        if (!spinner) return;
        spinner.classList.add('spinner-expanded');
        clearTimeout(collapseTimer);
        collapseTimer = null;
        if (!noCollapseTimer) {
          collapseTimer = setTimeout(function() {
            if (tooltip.classList.contains('schematic-spinner-dragging')) return;
            if (spinner.parentNode) spinner.classList.remove('spinner-expanded');
            collapseTimer = null;
          }, COLLAPSE_DELAY);
        }
      }

      tooltip._clearSpinnerCollapse = function() {
        clearTimeout(collapseTimer);
        collapseTimer = null;
      };

      function collapseSpinner(spinner) {
        if (!spinner) return;
        spinner.classList.remove('spinner-expanded');
        clearTimeout(collapseTimer);
        collapseTimer = null;
      }

      function setSpinnerValue(spinner, v, direction, noAnim) {
        v = Math.max(1, Math.min(999, v));
        var oldVal = parseInt(spinner.getAttribute('data-value'), 10) || 1;
        spinner.setAttribute('data-value', v);
        spinner.setAttribute('aria-valuenow', v);
        var valEl = spinner.querySelector('.tooltip-qty-value');
        var prevEl = spinner.querySelector('.tooltip-qty-prev');
        var nextEl = spinner.querySelector('.tooltip-qty-next');
        if (valEl) {
          valEl.setAttribute('data-value', v);
          valEl.textContent = v;
          if (!noAnim && direction != null) {
            valEl.classList.remove('spin-up', 'spin-down');
            valEl.offsetHeight;
            valEl.classList.add(direction === 1 ? 'spin-up' : 'spin-down');
            setTimeout(function() { valEl.classList.remove('spin-up', 'spin-down'); }, 160);
          }
        }
        if (prevEl) prevEl.textContent = v > 1 ? v - 1 : '';
        if (nextEl) nextEl.textContent = v < 999 ? v + 1 : '';
        if (v !== oldVal) triggerSpinnerChangeHaptic(spinner);
      }

      function endSpin(captureEl) {
        if (spinPtrId == null) return;
        try { if (captureEl && captureEl.releasePointerCapture) captureEl.releasePointerCapture(spinPtrId); } catch (err) {}
        spinPtrId = null;
        document.removeEventListener('pointermove', spinMoveHandler, true);
        document.removeEventListener('pointerup', spinUpHandler, true);
        document.removeEventListener('pointercancel', spinUpHandler, true);
      }

      tooltip.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(e) {
        var zone = e.target.closest('.tooltip-qty-spinner-zone');
        var spinner = zone ? zone.querySelector('.tooltip-qty-spinner') : e.target.closest('.tooltip-qty-spinner');
        if (!spinner) return;
        e.preventDefault();
        var captureEl = zone || spinner;
        try { if (captureEl.setPointerCapture) captureEl.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch (err) {}
        spinPtrId = e.pointerId;
        spinStartY = e.clientY;
        spinStartX = e.clientX;
        spinStartVal = parseInt(spinner.getAttribute('data-value'), 10) || 1;
        spinVelHistory = [{ y: e.clientY, t: Date.now() }];
        spinner.classList.add('spinner-dragging');
        tooltip.classList.add('schematic-spinner-dragging');
        document.body.classList.add('schematic-spinner-dragging');
        expandSpinner(spinner, true);

        spinMoveHandler = function(ev) {
          if (ev.pointerId !== spinPtrId) return;
          ev.preventDefault();
          var delta = ev.clientY - spinStartY;
          var pxPerStep = isMobile() ? PX_PER_STEP : 18;
          var steps = Math.round(-delta / pxPerStep);
          var newVal = Math.max(1, Math.min(999, spinStartVal + steps));
          var v = parseInt(spinner.getAttribute('data-value'), 10) || 1;
          if (newVal !== v) {
            setSpinnerValue(spinner, newVal, newVal > v ? 1 : -1, true);
          }
          spinVelHistory.push({ y: ev.clientY, t: Date.now() });
          if (spinVelHistory.length > 8) spinVelHistory.shift();
        };

        spinUpHandler = function(ev) {
          if (ev.pointerId !== spinPtrId) return;
          ev.preventDefault();
          var dx = ev.clientX - spinStartX;
          var dy = ev.clientY - spinStartY;
          var dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
          var vel = 0;
          if (spinVelHistory.length >= 2) {
            var last = spinVelHistory[spinVelHistory.length - 1];
            var prev = spinVelHistory[spinVelHistory.length - 2];
            var dt = Math.max(8, last.t - prev.t);
            vel = (prev.y - last.y) / dt;
          }
          spinner.classList.remove('spinner-dragging');
          tooltip.classList.remove('schematic-spinner-dragging');
          document.body.classList.remove('schematic-spinner-dragging');
          endSpin(captureEl);
          var clickThreshold = isMobile() ? 15 : CLICK_THRESHOLD;
          var didMove = spinVelHistory.length > 1;
          if (dist < clickThreshold && !didMove) {
            expandSpinner(spinner);
            if (spinner.classList.contains('spinner-expanded')) {
              var rect = spinner.getBoundingClientRect();
              var centerY = rect.top + rect.height / 2;
              var v = parseInt(spinner.getAttribute('data-value'), 10) || 1;
              if (ev.clientY < centerY) {
                setSpinnerValue(spinner, v + 1, 1);
              } else {
                setSpinnerValue(spinner, v - 1, -1);
              }
            }
            return;
          }
          expandSpinner(spinner);
          if (!isMobile()) return;
          if (Math.abs(vel) < 0.08) return;
          var v = parseInt(spinner.getAttribute('data-value'), 10) || 1;
          var absVel = Math.abs(vel);
          var inertia = vel * 14 * (1 + Math.min(absVel, 2) * 0.4);
          inertia = Math.max(-80, Math.min(80, inertia));
          if (Math.abs(inertia) < 0.5) return;
          (function runInertia() {
            var step = inertia > 0 ? 1 : -1;
            v += step;
            v = Math.max(1, Math.min(999, v));
            inertia *= 0.94;
            setSpinnerValue(spinner, v, step, true);
            if (Math.abs(inertia) >= 0.5) requestAnimationFrame(runInertia);
          })();
        };

        document.addEventListener('pointermove', spinMoveHandler, { capture: true });
        document.addEventListener('pointerup', spinUpHandler, { capture: true });
        document.addEventListener('pointercancel', spinUpHandler, { capture: true });
      });

      var wheelCursorTimeout;
      tooltip.addEventListener('wheel', function(e) {
        var zone = e.target.closest('.tooltip-qty-spinner-zone');
        var spinner = zone ? zone.querySelector('.tooltip-qty-spinner') : e.target.closest('.tooltip-qty-spinner');
        if (!spinner) return;
        expandSpinner(spinner);
        e.preventDefault();
        document.body.classList.add('schematic-spinner-scrolling');
        clearTimeout(wheelCursorTimeout);
        wheelCursorTimeout = setTimeout(function() {
          document.body.classList.remove('schematic-spinner-scrolling');
        }, 350);
        var v = parseInt(spinner.getAttribute('data-value'), 10) || 1;
        v += e.deltaY > 0 ? -1 : 1;
        setSpinnerValue(spinner, v, e.deltaY > 0 ? -1 : 1);
      }, { passive: false });

      tooltip.addEventListener('mouseover', function(e) {
        if (isMobile()) return;
        var zone = e.target.closest('.tooltip-qty-spinner-zone');
        var spinner = zone ? zone.querySelector('.tooltip-qty-spinner') : e.target.closest('.tooltip-qty-spinner');
