        if (spinner) expandSpinner(spinner);
      });

      tooltip.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
        var spinner = e.target.closest('.tooltip-qty-spinner');
        if (!spinner || e.target !== spinner) return;
        expandSpinner(spinner);
        var v = parseInt(spinner.getAttribute('data-value'), 10) || 1;
        if (e.key === 'ArrowUp') {
          e.preventDefault();
          setSpinnerValue(spinner, v + 1, 1);
        } else if (e.key === 'ArrowDown') {
          e.preventDefault();
          setSpinnerValue(spinner, v - 1, -1);
        } else return;
      });
    })();

    tooltip.addEventListener('change', function(e) {
      var t = e.target;
      if (!t || !t.classList || !t.classList.contains('tooltip-variant-select')) return;
      syncModalVariantUi(tooltip);
    });

    tooltip.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
      if (e.target.closest('.tooltip-close, [data-tooltip-dismiss]')) {
        e.preventDefault();
        triggerTapHaptic();
        closeMobileTooltip();
        return;
      }
      if (e.target.closest('.tooltip-qty-spinner-zone') || e.target.closest('.tooltip-qty-spinner')) return;
      var addBtn = e.target.closest('.tooltip-btn-add');
      if (addBtn) {
        if (addBtn.disabled) return;
        e.preventDefault();
        triggerTapHaptic();
        var vid = addBtn.getAttribute('data-variant-id');
        var valEl = tooltip.querySelector('.tooltip-qty-value');
        var qty = 1;
        if (valEl) {
          qty = parseInt(valEl.getAttribute('data-value'), 10) || 1;
          qty = Math.max(1, Math.min(999, qty));
        }
        if (vid) quickAddToCart(parseInt(vid, 10), qty);
      }
    });

    function quickAddToCart(variantId, quantity) {
      quantity = quantity || 1;
      if (tooltip.classList.contains('tooltip-mobile')) closeMobileTooltip();
      showCartFeedback(quantity);
      fetch('/cart/add.js', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ id: variantId, quantity: quantity })
      }).then(function(r) {
        return r.json();
      }).then(function(data) {
        if (data.message) {
          showCartFeedbackFail(data.message);
        }
        if (typeof document.dispatchEvent === 'function') {
          document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('cart:refresh'));
        }
      }).catch(function(err) {
        showCartFeedbackFail('Could not add to cart.');
      });
    }
  })();
</script>


</div>

1.983.154.854.197 1.783.218 2.58.013.45.019.9.02 1.36.005.557.005 1.113.005 1.671v76.318c0 .558 0 1.114-.004 1.682-.002.45-.008.9-.02 1.35-.022.796-.065 1.725-.221 2.589a6.855 6.855 0 0 1-.645 1.975 6.397 6.397 0 0 1-2.808 2.807c-.6.306-1.228.511-1.971.645-.881.157-1.847.2-2.574.22-.457.01-.912.017-1.379.019-.555.004-1.113.004-1.669.004H14.801c-.55 0-1.1 0-1.66-.004a74.993 74.993 0 0 2-8.395 5.1-3.598 6.2-.95 15.4 2.55 20.45 1.699 2.5 3.747 5.25 6.445 5.151 2.55-.1 3.549-1.65 6.647-1.65 3.097 0 3.997 1.65 6.696 1.6 2.798-.05 4.548-2.5 6.247-5 1.95-2.85 2.747-5.6 2.797-5.75-.05-.05-5.396-2.101-5.446-8.251-.05-5.15 4.198-7.6 4.398-7.751-2.399-3.548-6.147-3.948-7.447-4.048" /></g><g><path fill="#000" d="M78.973 32.11c7.278 0 12.347 5.017 12.347 12.321 0 7.33-5.173 12.373-12.529 12.373h-8.058V69.62h-5.822V32.11h14.062zm-8.24 19.807h6.68c5.07 0 7.954-2.729 7.954-7.46 0-4.73-2.885-7.434-7.928-7.434h-6.706v14.894z" /><path fill="#000" d="M92.764 61.847c0-4.809 3.665-7.564 10.423-7.98l7.252-.442v-2.08c0-3.04-2.001-4.704-5.562-4.704-2.938 0-5.07 1.507-5.51 3.82h-5.252c.157-4.86 4.731-8.395 10.918-8.395 6.654 0 10.995 3.483 10.995 8.89v18.663h-5.38v-4.497h-.13c-1.534 2.937-4.914 4.782-8.579 4.782-5.406 0-9.175-3.222-9.175-8.057zm17.675-2.417v-2.106l-6.472.416c-3.64.234-5.536 1.585-5.536 3.95 0 2.288 1.975 3.77 5.068 3.77 3.95 0 6.94-2.522 6.94-6.03z" /><path fill="#000" d="M120.975 79.652v-4.496c.364.051 1.247.103 1.715.103 2.573 0 4.029-1.09 4.913-3.899l.52-1.663-9.852-27.293h6.082l6.863 22.146h.13l6.862-22.146h5.927l-10.216 28.67c-2.34 6.577-5.017 8.735-10.683 8.735-.442 0-1.872-.052-2.261-.157z" /></g></g></svg>
</figure><figure><svg class="payment-icons__icon" viewBox="0 0 38 24" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" role="img" width="38" height="24" aria-labelledby="pi-diners_club"><title
